We can implement a generic local optimizer:
```
def proj_grad_optimize(a, deadline, rng):
    a=norm(a)
    best=a.copy(); bestM=scoreM(a)
    beta_temp=10; no=0
    while time<deadline:
        b=conv(a); M=b.max()
        update best
        weights = softmax_weights(b, temp=?)
        G=grad_weighted(a,b,weights)
        Gc=G-G.mean()
        if norm(Gc)<1e-14: break
        # make v
        step0 = max(a) / (np.linalg.norm(Gc)+eps) maybe >0
        v = a - step0 * Gc
        v = clip normalize
        # if v too close to a, also FW vertex
        # line search over t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]
        cands = [a, (1-t)a+t v?]
        best cand by M; if improved accept; if best is v t? 
        if no improve: perturb a (normal sigma) and maybe change beta
```

```
def project_simplex(v):
    x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(x) - 1
    ind = np.arange(n) + 1
    cond = x - cssv / ind > 0
    i = cond.nonzero()[0][-1]
    theta = cssv[i] / (i+1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta,0)
```
This preserves sum≈1. Use this after step. It is more stable.

```
def coordinate_sweep(a, b, mode, rng, deadline, n_candidates=24, use_max=True):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2*n - 1
    # assume a.sum()≈1
    for m in rng.permutation(n):
       if time out: break
       x0=a[m]
       # candidate xs
       scale = max(x0, 1.0/n, 1e-12)
       # Generate unique sorted within [0, upper]
       # Need avoid S_new=0.
       upper = max(10*scale, 0.5) maybe? For n=192, 1/n=0.005, 10scale=0.05. But some coefficients might be 0.1; scale covers. Upper maybe 1.0? Candidate 1.0 huge but evaluate.
       # Include large: 0.2,0.5,1.0 if upper>... but many.
```

```
def _project_simplex? maybe for gradient? Not if coordinate.

def _coordinate_refine(a, b, deadline, rng, best, modes=None):
   n=len(a); L=2n-1
   a=a.copy(); b=b.copy(); a=_normalize? b=conv_self(a)
   local_best_score=...
   stagn=0
   sweep=0
   sigma=0.1
   while time<deadline:
      # choose mode sequence
      mode = modes[sweep % len(modes)]
      # do a sweep
      ord = rng.permutation(n)
      improved_this_sweep=False
      for m in ord:
         if time>deadline: break
         old = a[m]
         xs = _make_xs(old,n)
         # build Lvec
         L=2*n-1? variable len_b
         Lvec=np.zeros(L); ...
         # objective; choose best x
         if best val < old val - 1e-15:
             # update a,b
             newa = a.copy()? Update in place efficient
             a[m]=xbest
             S=1-old+xbest
             # b = (b + ...)/S^2 using vector for xbest
             b[lo:hi] += (xbest-old)*Lvec[lo:hi]
             if em>=0: b[em]+=xbest*xbest-old*old
             a/=S; b/=S*S
             # fix sums
             sa=a.sum(); sb=b.sum()? sum b should1; if not scale.
             # recompute exact score fast
             score = 2*n*b.max()
             if score < best['score'] - 1e-12:
                 # confirm with exact evaluator soon
                 exact = evaluate_sequence(...)
                 if finite and exact<best score: update best seq = a.copy()
             improved_this_sweep=True
      sweep++
      if not improved_this_sweep: stagn++; else stagn=0
      if stagn>3:
         # perturb
         a = _normalize(np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0, sigma*np.mean(a), n), 0))
         b = _conv_self(a)
         sigma = min(1.0, sigma*1.5)
         stagn=0
      # every 200 sweeps recompute b
      if sweep%200==0: b=_conv_self(a); a=_normalize(a)
   # return a,b
```

```
def _f2_lnbfgs(a0, deadline, maxiter=300):
   a0_pos = np.maximum(a0,1e-12); a0_pos/=sum
   theta0 = np.log(a0_pos)
   # center? theta0 -= mean
   n=len(a0)
   nfft = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   def fun_grad(theta):
      th = theta - theta.mean()
      m=th.max(); z=np.exp(th-m); a=z/z.sum()
      b=_conv_self(a)
      val=float(np.dot(b,b))
      w=2*b
      G=_grad_weighted(a,w)  # gradient of F2
      # grad_theta = (a[:,None] - np.outer(a,a)) @ G
      # Compute efficiently: grad_theta = a*G - a*(a@G)
      grad_theta = a*G - a*(np.dot(a,G))
      # tiny ridge to break translation? grad_theta already sum0. Add 1e-12*theta centered? The null direction gradient0, L-BFGS can have issues. Add reg = 1e-10*(th-th.mean())
      grad_theta = grad_theta + 1e-12*(th - th.mean())? But objective invariant; adding tiny reg changes. Use reg=1e-10.
      return val, grad_theta
   res = minimize(fun_grad, theta0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':..., 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':40})
```

FFT gradient formula from earlier:
```
def _grad_w_fft(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w)
    # w length 2n-1; but if w from b length L. Ensure.
    nfft=next_fast_len(n+L-1)
    A=rfft(a,nfft)
    Wr=rfft(w[::-1],nfft)
    h=irfft(A*Wr,nfft, n+L-1)  # length 3n-2
    h = h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return 2*h
```
Need `irfft_fft` returns real length nfft; slicing first `n+L-1` values. `scipy.fft.irfft(x, n=n+L-1)` returns length n+L-1. Good.
Test with small? For n=2 earlier, h slice correct.
Roundoff; good.
Could also direct if n small to avoid FFT overhead? For n=192, direct maybe faster? But FFT okay.

```
def _lp_dir(a, M=None):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if M is None: M=_conv_self(a).max()
   K=np.arange(L)[:,None]
   J=np.arange(n)[None,:]
   valid=(K>=0)&(K<n)?? Actually need k-j in [0,n-1]: 0<=K-J<n.
   valid=(K-J>=0)&(K-J<n)
   src=np.clip(K-J,0,n-1)
   A=np.zeros((L,n)); A[valid]=a[src[valid]]
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
   if success:
      g=res.x; if sum>1e-12: return g/g.sum()
   return None
```

L-BFGS objective F2 uses `val=dot(b,b)`, `grad_theta = a*G - a*(a@G)`. G is gradient of F2 = `grad_w(a, 2*b)`. Our `_grad_w_fft(a,2*b)` returns 2*shifted weights. Let's verify: For F2 = ∑ b_k^2, weights c=2b, gradient = ∑ c_k ∂b_k = grad_w(a,c), where grad_w returns 2∑ c_k a_shift. If c=2b, gradient =4∑b a_shift. Our `_grad_w_fft` with w=c returns 2*... = correct. Good.
Could use direct gradient for L-BFGS maybe. Add small Hessian? no.

Potential null direction: We add `grad_theta += 1e-10*(th - th.mean())` to make Hessian positive on null? But objective invariant, adding reg changes objective? We must add corresponding objective `0.5*1e-10*||th-mean||^2`? If gradient includes reg but objective doesn't, inconsistency may cause issues. Better not add reg; L-BFGS can handle? The gradient is orthogonal to null, but the secant on null might be zero. Could be okay. To avoid drift, center theta inside fun before computing; the points along null are equivalent, but L-BFGS may step along null because gradient zero? It shouldn't, but Hessian approx may. We can after each iteration? Not possible. Could fix translation by reducing parameter dimension: set theta[0]=0 or center during objective and setter? In L-BFGS, if objective depends only on centered theta, gradient also centered; the Hessian has null but L-BFGS usually okay. We can set `theta0[0]=0` and in fun, `th = theta; th = th - th.mean()` (thus effectively projects). The optimizer may change theta[0] but objective ignores. It's okay.

Detailed `_coordinate_refine` code:

```
def _coordinate_refine(a, b, deadline, rng, best, lam=0.25, min_chunk=0.2):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0: return
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    b = _conv_self(a)
    L = 2*n - 1
    modes = ['f2','merit','max','merit','f2']
    sweep = 0
    exact_stagn = 0
    sigma = 0.05
    last_exact = 0.0
    cur_best_local = None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # recompute at sweep start
        a = _normalize(a)
        b = _conv_self(a)
        mode = modes[sweep % len(modes)]
        old_obj = None? We'll compute inside known.
        old_max = float(np.max(b))
        old_f2 = float(np.dot(b,b))
        mode old obj:
          if mode f2 old_obj=old_f2; max old_obj=old_max; merit old_obj=old_max+lam*old_f2
        improved=False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for m in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            x0 = float(a[m])
            xs = _candidate_xs(x0, n)
            if xs.size == 0: continue
            S = 1.0 - x0 + xs
            valid = S > 1e-12
            if not valid.any(): continue
            xs=xs[valid]; S=S[valid]
            Lvec = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            lo = m
            hi = min(L, m+n)
            if lo < hi:
                Lvec[lo:hi] = 2.0 * a[lo-m:hi-m]
            em = 2*m
            if em < L:
                Lvec[em] = 0.0
            # compute vals
            diff = (xs - x0)[:,None]
            # We can compute bnew for candidates
            if mode == 'f2' or mode == 'merit':
                bnew = b[None,:] + diff*Lvec[None,:]
            else:
                bnew = b[None,:] + diff*Lvec[None,:]  # same
            if em < L:
                bnew[:,em] += (xs*xs - x0*x0)[:,None]
            # if bnew negative? maybe skip clip
            invS2 = 1.0/(S*S)
            if mode == 'max':
                vals = np.max(bnew, axis=1) * invS2
            elif mode == 'f2':
                vals = np.einsum('ij,ij->i', bnew, bnew) * invS2 * invS2
            else:
                maxr = np.max(bnew, axis=1) * invS2
                f2r = np.einsum('ij,ij->i', bnew, bnew) * invS2 * invS2
                vals = maxr + lam * f2r
            vals = np.where(valid, vals, np.inf)
            # if all inf skip
            if not np.isfinite(vals).any(): continue
            idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
            best_val = float(vals[idx])
            # refine around idx
            if 0 <= idx < xs.size:
                xm = xs[idx]
                left = xs[idx-1] if idx>0 else max(0.0, xm*0.9)
                right = xs[idx+1] if idx<xs.size-1 else min(1.0, xm*1.1)
                # Also include original x0? maybe
                if right <= left:
                    right = min(1.0, left + max(1e-6, 0.01*max(1.0/n, left)))
                if right > left and (right-left) > 1e-12:
                    fine = np.linspace(left, right, 6)
                    # evaluate fine with same Lvec
                    S2 = 1.0 - x0 + fine; valid2=S2>1e-12
                    if valid2.any():
                       fine=fine[valid2]; S2=S2[valid2]
                       bfin = b[None,:] + (fine[:,None]-x0)*Lvec[None,:]
                       if em < L: bfin[:,em] += (fine*fine - x0*x0)[:,None]
                       inv2=1/(S2*S2)
                       if mode max... similar
                       idx2=argmin
                       if val2 < best_val: update xm/best_val/idx_best_fine
```

```
def _eval_coord_values(b, Lvec, em, x0, xs, mode, lam):
    S=1-x0+xs; valid=S>1e-12
    if not valid.any(): return np.full(len(xs),np.inf)
    d=np.nonzero? Use all xs with inf for invalid to keep simple? For fine maybe.
    bnew = b[None,:] + (xs[:,None]-x0)*Lvec[None,:]
    if em>=0: bnew[:,em] += xs*xs - x0*x0
    invS2 = np.where(valid, 1/(S*S), 0)
    maxr = np.max(bnew, axis=1)*invS2
    f2r = np.einsum('ij,ij->i', bnew,bnew)*invS2*invS2
    if mode == 'max': vals=maxr
    elif mode == 'f2': vals=f2r
    else: vals=maxr+lam*f2r
    vals=np.where(valid, vals, np.inf)
    return vals
```

Now, seed creation:
```
def _seed_from_parent(parent, n, rng, sigma, use_sym=False):
   if parent.size == n: a=parent.copy()
   else: a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,parent.size), parent)
   if use_sym: a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   mean = max(a.mean(),1e-9)
   if sigma>0: a += rng.normal(0, sigma*mean, n)
   return _normalize(a)
```

Let's write exact update helper:
```
def _try_update_best(seq, best, min_score=0.0):
    sc = _exact_score(seq)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best['score'] - 1e-12:
```

```
def _grad_w(a,w):
   n=len(a); L=len(w)
   # direct O(nL)
   # using convolution via np.convolve might be faster and simpler
   try:
      rr = w[::-1]
      full = np.convolve(a, rr, mode='full')
      h = full[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      return 2*h
   except:
      h=np.zeros(n)
      for k in range(L):
         wk=w[k]
         if wk==0: continue
         lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
         # gradient contribution 2*wk*a[k-j]? We can vectorize: j=lo..hi, grad[j]+=2*wk*a[k-j]
         j=np.arange(lo,hi+1); h[j]+=2*wk*a[k-j]
      return h
```

```
def _grad_w_fft(a,w):
  n=len(a); L=len(w); nfft=next_fast_len(n+L-1)
  A=rfft(a,nfft); Wr=rfft(w[::-1],nfft); h=irfft(A*Wr,nfft, n+L-1)
  return 2*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

L-BFGS `_lbfgs_f2(a0, deadline, best)`:
```
def _lbfgs_f2(a0, deadline, best):
    if minimize is None: return
    n=len(a0)
    if n>512 or deadline-time.monotonic()<15: return
    a0p=np.maximum(a0,1e-12); a0p=a0p/a0p.sum()
    th0=np.log(a0p); th0-=th0.mean()
    sub_dead = deadline - 5
    best_local_a=a0p.copy(); best_local_fast=2*n*_conv_self(a0p).max()
    def fun_jac(theta):
        nonlocal best_local_a, best_local_fast
        th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=float) - np.mean(theta)
        mx = th.max(); z=np.exp(th-mx); a=z/z.sum()
        b=_conv_self(a)
        val=float(np.dot(b,b))
        fast=2*n*float(b.max())
        if fast < best_local_fast - 1e-12:
            best_local_fast=fast; best_local_a=a.copy()
        G=_grad_w(a, 2*b)
        grad = a*G - a*float(np.dot(a,G))
        # symmetrize? grad sum ~0
        return val, grad
    stop={'done':False}
    def cb(k):
       if time.monotonic()>sub_dead:
          stop['done']=True
       return stop['done']
    try:
       res = minimize(fun_jac, th0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':200, 'maxls':30, 'gtol':1e-10}, callback=cb)
    except Exception:
       res=None
    # evaluate best_local exact
    if np.isfinite(best_local_fast) and best_local_fast < best['score']:
       _try_update_best(best_local_a,best)
```
If timeout after flag, `minimize` might return with partial; we still evaluate best local found. Good.

But `minimize` with `jac=True` and fun_jac returning tuple; ensure first return is float. Yes.

```
def _lp_polish(a0, deadline, best, max_iters=3):
   n=len(a0)
   if linprog is None or n>512 or deadline-now<30: return
   a=_normalize(a0.copy()); b=_conv_self(a)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if now > deadline: break
      M=float(b.max())
      target = M  # maybe max(M*0.98? if stagnant)
      A = _build_A(a)
      res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=n*target, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=...)
      if not success: break
      h=res.x; sh=h.sum()
      if not finite or sh<1e-8: break
      g=h/sh
      # mix g with uniform maybe
      g = 0.9*g + 0.1/n
      # line search
      best_local=a.copy(); best_M=M
      for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]:
          hvec=(1-t)*a+t*g; hvec=_normalize(np.maximum(hvec,0)); hb=_conv_self(hvec); mh=float(hb.max()); if mh<best_M: best_M=mh; best_local=hvec
      if best_M < M*(1-1e-10):
          a=best_local; b=_conv_self(a)
          if _fast<best: _try_update
      else:
          break
```
Build A:
```
def _build_A(a):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   K=np.arange(L)[:,None]; J=np.arange(n)[None,:]
   valid = (K - J >= 0) & (K - J < n)
   src = np.clip(K - J, 0, n-1).astype(np.intp)
   A = np.zeros((L,n)); A[valid]=a[src[valid]]
   return A
```

```
def _grad_w(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w)
    try:
       nfft=next_fast_len(n+L-1)
       A=rfft(a,nfft)
       Wr=rfft(w[::-1], nfft)
       h=irfft(A*Wr, nfft, n+L-1)
       return 2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    except Exception:
       # direct
       full=np.convolve(a, w[::-1], mode='full')
       return 2.0*full[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    rfft = None
    irfft = None
    next_fast_len = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None


def _fft_size(m):
    if next_fast_len is not None:
        try:
            return int(next_fast_len(int(m)))
        except Exception:
            pass
    m = int(m)
    s = 1
    while s < m:
        s <<= 1
    return s


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    if rfft is not None:
        try:
            fs = _fft_size(L)
            F = rfft(a, fs)
            b = irfft(F * F, fs, L)
            b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)[:L]
            np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
            b[~np.isfinite(b)] = 0.0
            return b
        except Exception:
            pass
    b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)[:L]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    b[~np.isfinite(b)] = 0.0
    return b


def _fast_score(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    b = _conv_self(a)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if a.size == 0:
            return math.inf
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _try_update_best(seq, best):
    seq = _normalize(seq)
    sc = _exact_score(seq)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best["score"] - 1e-12:
        best["score"] = float(sc)
        best["seq"] = seq.copy()
        return True
    return False


def _get_parent():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(p).all():
        p = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(p.sum()) <= 1e-14:
        p = np.ones(192, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _parent_at_length(parent, n, rng=None, sigma=0.0):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if parent.size <= 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif parent.size == n:
        a = parent.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    else:
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent.size)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.interp(x1, x0, parent)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if sigma > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        mean = max(float(a.mean()), 1e-12)
        a = a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma * mean, size=n)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _random_seed(n, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if kind == 0:
        u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(2, 6))):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2 + 1)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            u += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * i / n + ph)
        a = np.exp(0.9 * (u - u.mean()))
    elif kind == 1:
        u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(2, 5))):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2 + 1)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            u += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * i / n + ph)
        a = np.exp(0.7 * u + rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5) * i / max(1.0, n))
    elif kind == 2:
        a = 1.0 + 0.75 * np.sin(math.pi * i / max(1.0, n) * float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0)))
        a += 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i * float(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 2 + 1))) / n)
    elif kind == 3:
        a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) ** float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.0)) + 1e-3
    elif kind == 4:
        a = np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * i / max(1.0, n))
        a += float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.7)) * np.exp(-((i - float(rng.uniform(0.0, n))) ** 2) / (2.0 * max(1.0, float(n) * 0.1) ** 2))
    else:
        a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.05
        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    return _normalize(a)


def _candidate_xs(x0, n, max_xs=22):
    base = max(float(x0), 1.0 / float(max(1, n)), 1e-12)
    factors = np.array(
        [0.0, 0.03, 0.08, 0.16, 0.27, 0.42, 0.58, 0.73, 0.87, 1.0,
         1.12, 1.27, 1.45, 1.67, 1.95, 2.31, 2.8, 3.5, 4.4, 5.6, 7.1, 9.0],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )
    xs = base * factors
    extra = np.array(
        [1.0 / n, 3.0 / n, 8.0 / n, 18.0 / n, 40.0 / n, 0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.45, 0.7],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )
    xs = np.concatenate((xs, extra))
    xs = np.where(np.isfinite(xs), xs, 0.0)
    xs = np.unique(xs[(xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0)])
    if xs.size > max_xs:
        qx = np.quantile(xs, np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max_xs))
        xs = np.unique(qx)
    if xs.size == 0:
        xs = np.array([0.0])
    return xs


def _eval_coord_values(b, Lvec, em, x0, xs, mode, lam):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = b.size
    S = 1.0 - x0 + xs
    valid = S > 1e-12
    vals = np.full(xs.size, math.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    if xs.size == 0 or not np.any(valid):
        return vals

    if mode == "max":
        bnew = b[None, :] + (xs[:, None] - x0) * Lvec[None, :]
        if 0 <= em < L:
            bnew[:, em] += xs * xs - x0 * x0
        invS2 = np.zeros(xs.size, dtype=np.float64)
        invS2[valid] = 1.0 / (S[valid] * S[valid])
        vals = np.max(bnew, axis=1) * invS2
    elif mode == "f2":
        bnew = b[None, :] + (xs[:, None] - x0) * Lvec[None, :]
        if 0 <= em < L:
            bnew[:, em] += xs * xs - x0 * x0
        invS2 = np.zeros(xs.size, dtype=np.float64)
        invS2[valid] = 1.0 / (S[valid] * S[valid])
        vals = np.einsum("ij,ij->i", bnew, bnew) * invS2 * invS2
    else:
        bnew = b[None, :] + (xs[:, None] - x0) * Lvec[None, :]
        if 0 <= em < L:
            bnew[:, em] += xs * xs - x0 * x0
        invS2 = np.zeros(xs.size, dtype=np.float64)
        invS2[valid] = 1.0 / (S[valid] * S[valid])
        maxr = np.max(bnew, axis=1) * invS2
        f2r = np.einsum("ij,ij->i", bnew, bnew) * invS2 * invS2
        vals = maxr + lam * f2r

    vals = np.where(valid, vals, math.inf)
    vals[~np.isfinite(vals)] = math.inf
    return vals


def _coordinate_refine(a, b, deadline, rng, best, lam=0.30):
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    b = _conv_self(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n < 2:
        _try_update_best(a, best)
        return

    modes = ["f2", "merit", "max", "merit", "f2", "max"]
    sweep = 0
    exact_stagn = 0
    sigma = 0.05
    last_exact = 0.0

    if _try_update_best(a, best):
        last_exact = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = _normalize(a)
        b = _conv_self(a)
        mode = modes[sweep % len(modes)]

        old_max = float(np.max(b))
        old_f2 = float(np.dot(b, b))
        if mode == "max":
            old_obj = old_max
        elif mode == "f2":
            old_obj = old_f2
        else:
            old_obj = old_max + lam * old_f2

        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)

        for m in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            x0 = float(a[m])
            xs = _candidate_xs(x0, n, max_xs=24 if n <= 256 else 14)
            if xs.size == 0:
                continue

            Lvec = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            lo = m
            hi = min(L, m + n)
            if lo < hi:
                Lvec[lo:hi] = 2.0 * a[lo - m: hi - m]
            em = 2 * m
            if em < L:
                Lvec[em] = 0.0

            vals = _eval_coord_values(b, Lvec, em, x0, xs, mode, lam)
            if not np.isfinite(vals).any():
                continue
            idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
            best_val = float(vals[idx])
            xb = float(xs[idx])

            if np.isfinite(best_val):
                left = float(xs[idx - 1]) if idx > 0 else max(0.0, xb * 0.85)
                right = float(xs[idx + 1]) if idx < xs.size - 1 else min(1.0, xb * 1.15 if xb > 0 else 1.0 / n)
                if right > left + 1e-12:
                    fine = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.array([xb]), np.linspace(left, right, 8))))
                    fine = fine[(fine >= 0.0) & (fine <= 1.0)]
                    if fine.size:
                        fvals = _eval_coord_values(b, Lvec, em, x0, fine, mode, lam)
                        if np.isfinite(fvals).any():
                            fidx = int(np.argmin(fvals))
                            if float(fvals[fidx]) < best_val:
                                best_val = float(fvals[fidx])
                                xb = float(fine[fidx])

            if np.isfinite(best_val) and best_val < old_obj * (1.0 - 1e-14) - 1e-16:
                Snew = 1.0 - x0 + xb
                if Snew > 1e-12:
                    b = b + (xb - x0) * Lvec
                    if em < L:
                        b[em] = b[em] + xb * xb - x0 * x0
                    a[m] = xb
                    a /= Snew
                    b /= (Snew * Snew)

                    sa = float(a.sum())
                    if sa > 1e-14:
                        a /= sa
                        b /= (sa * sa)
                    sb = float(b.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(sb) and sb > 1e-14 and abs(sb - 1.0) > 1e-7:
                        b = _conv_self(a)
                    else:
                        if np.isfinite(sb) and sb > 0.0:
                            b /= sb
                        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
                        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
                    if b.size and float(np.min(b)) < -1e-9:
                        b = _conv_self(a)
                    improved = True

        sweep += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        fast = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))
        if (fast < best["score"] + 1e-8) and (now - last_exact > 0.65 or exact_stagn >= 3):
            if _try_update_best(a, best):
                last_exact = now
                exact_stagn = 0
            else:
                exact_stagn += 1

        if (not improved) or exact_stagn >= 7:
            mean_a = max(float(a.mean()), 1e-12)
            if sigma < 0.35 and exact_stagn >= 3:
                a = _normalize(np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma * mean_a, n), 0.0))
            else:
                a = a.copy()
                for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 5))):
                    mj = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                    xj = float(a[mj])
                    cands = _candidate_xs(xj, n, max_xs=12)
                    if cands.size:
                        a[mj] = float(cands[int(rng.integers(0, cands.size))])
                a = _normalize(np.maximum(a, 0.0))
                if rng.random() < 0.35:
                    a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                    a = _normalize(a)
            sigma = min(1.0, sigma * 1.35)
            if sigma > 0.6 and exact_stagn >= 8:
                sigma = 0.08
            b = _conv_self(a)
            exact_stagn = 0

        if _try_update_best(a, best):
            break

    _try_update_best(a, best)


def _grad_w(a, w):
    n = len(a)
    L = len(w)
    try:
        fs = _fft_size(n + L - 1)
        A = rfft(a, fs)
        W = rfft(w[::-1], fs)
        h = irfft(A * W, fs, n + L - 1)
        return 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    except Exception:
        full = np.convolve(a, w[::-1], mode="full")
        return 2.0 * full[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def _lbfgs_f2(a0, deadline, best):
    if minimize is None:
        return
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    if n > 512 or n < 2:
        return
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
        return

    a0 = np.maximum(a0, 1e-12)
    a0 = a0 / a0.sum()
    theta0 = np.log(a0).astype(np.float64)
    theta0 -= theta0.mean()

    local = {
        "a": a0.copy(),
        "fast": _fast_score(a0),
    }

    def fun_jac(theta):
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        theta = theta - theta.mean()
        mx = float(theta.max())
        z = np.exp(theta - mx)
        a = z / z.sum()
        b = _conv_self(a)
        val = float(np.dot(b, b))
        fast = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))
        if fast < local["fast"] - 1e-13:
            local["fast"] = float(fast)
            local["a"] = a.copy()
        G = _grad_w(a, 2.0 * b)
        grad = a * G - a * float(np.dot(a, G))
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        return val, grad

    stop = {"done": False}

    def cb(k):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            stop["done"] = True
        return stop["done"]

    try:
        minimize(
            fun_jac,
            theta0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={"maxiter": 220, "maxls": 30, "gtol": 1e-10},
            callback=cb,
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    if np.isfinite(local["fast"]) and local["fast"] < best["score"] + 1e-8:
        _try_update_best(local["a"], best)


def _build_lp_A(a):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    K = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
    J = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
    D = K - J
    valid = (D >= 0) & (D < n)
    src = np.clip(D, 0, n - 1).astype(np.intp)
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A[valid] = a[src[valid]]
    return A


def _lp_polish(a0, deadline, best, max_iters=3):
    if linprog is None:
        return
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    if n > 512 or n < 2:
        return
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
        return

    a = a0.copy()
    b = _conv_self(a)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            return
        try:
            A = _build_lp_A(a)
            rhs = n * M
            res = linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=rhs,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": 4.0, "presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            return

        if getattr(res, "success", False) is not True:
            break
        h = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if h.size != n or not np.isfinite(h).all():
            break
        sh = float(h.sum())
        if sh <= 1e-10:
            break
        g = h / sh
        g = 0.88 * g + 0.12 * (1.0 / n)

        best_loc = a.copy()
        best_M = M
        for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            cand = _normalize(np.maximum(cand, 0.0))
            bc = _conv_self(cand)
            mc = float(np.max(bc))
            if mc < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = mc
                best_loc = cand.copy()
                b = bc

        if best_M < M * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            a = best_loc
            if 2.0 * float(n) * best_M < best["score"] + 1e-7:
                _try_update_best(a, best)
        else:
            break

    if 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b)) < best["score"] + 1e-7:
        _try_update_best(a, best)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, budget - 18.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = _get_parent()
    parent_norm = _normalize(parent)

    best = {
        "score": math.inf,
        "seq": parent_norm.copy(),
    }
    if not _try_update_best(parent_norm, best):
        hb = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        if _try_update_best(hb, best):
            pass
        else:
            hb = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            best = {"score": _fast_score(hb), "seq": hb.copy()}

    n_parent = int(best["seq"].size)
    if n_parent < 16:
        n_parent = 64

    trial_deadline = max(t0 + 5.0, hard_deadline - (120.0 if budget > 600.0 else 45.0))

    trials = []
    for sig in (0.0, 0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.7):
        trials.append((n_parent, "parent", sig, 0))
    for k in range(14):
        trials.append((n_parent, "random", 0, k))

    other_ns = []
    for n in (n_parent + 32, n_parent + 64, 128, 160, 200, 224, 256, 320, 384):
        if 32 <= n <= 512 and n not in other_ns:
            other_ns.append(n)
    for n in other_ns[:7]:
        trials.append((n, "parent", 0.0, 0))
        trials.append((n, "random", 0, int(n)))

    ti = 0
    while time.monotonic() < trial_deadline - 12.0:
        if ti >= len(trials):
            nn = int(rng.integers(max(32, n_parent // 2), min(513, 3 * n_parent + 1)))
            trials.append((nn, "random", 0, int(rng.integers(0, 10**9))))

        n, kind, sig, _x = trials[ti]
        ti += 1
        if n < 16:
            continue

        rem = trial_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 15.0:
            break

        if ti <= 2:
            chunk = min(160.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.35))
        elif ti <= 10:
            chunk = min(85.0, max(15.0, rem * 0.22))
        else:
            chunk = min(45.0, max(10.0, rem * 0.13))

        if kind == "parent":
            a = _parent_at_length(parent, n, rng=rng, sigma=float(sig))
        else:
            a = _random_seed(n, rng)
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                ap = _parent_at_length(parent, n, rng=None, sigma=0.0)
                a = (1.0 - 0.35) * a + 0.35 * ap
                a = _normalize(a)

        b = _conv_self(a)
        if 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b)) < best["score"] + 1e-8:
            _try_update_best(a, best)

        _coordinate_refine(a, b, min(trial_deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + chunk), rng, best)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 15.0:
        a = _normalize(best["seq"].copy())
        b = _conv_self(a)
        fin1 = max(time.monotonic(), hard_deadline - 90.0)
        _coordinate_refine(a, b, fin1, rng, best)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 35.0:
        _lbfgs_f2(best["seq"], hard_deadline - 25.0, best)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20.0:
        _lp_polish(best["seq"], hard_deadline - 12.0, best, max_iters=2)

    final_seq = _normalize(best["seq"])
    final_score = _exact_score(final_seq)

    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score <= 0.0:
        fb = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        fs = _exact_score(fb)
        if np.isfinite(fs):
            final_seq = fb
        else:
            final_seq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    final_seq = np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_seq.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(final_seq).all() or final_seq.sum() <= 1e-12:
        final_seq = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
